- **claude-jarvis**: Audio + voice notifications for Claude Code on macOS: a chime and a short spoken Jarvis-style summary when a turn finishes, plus cues for subagent completion, milestones (push, merge, tests, build), and command failures. - **claude-jev**: Five hooks, one batched API call each. Classifies every prompt into a one-line routing hint; sets the model tier a subagent spawns on; judges each edit against the rules already in your CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md and .claude/rules, citing the one it breaks; and on compaction judges 150 transcript blocks keep, truncate or drop so the survivors carry over verbatim instead of through a generated summary. Fails open everywhere. Shaped by evals over 2,464 real prompts and 250 real edits; see eval/. ([0x7067/claude-jev](https://github.com/0x7067/claude-jev) · ★27 · 4 skills, 4 hook files)
